# Agents · C · part 10

960 entries, CommandCenter to compliance-officer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **CommandCenter**: Self-anneal agent for the CommandCenter orchestration platform. Edit code, run tests, debug issues, review agent repos, and improve the CC platform itself. Full access to the CommandCenter codebase. Use when working on the CommandCenter platform, its gateway, orchestrator, agent loader, skills packages, or chat frontend. ([FracktalWorks/CommandCenter](https://github.com/FracktalWorks/CommandCenter/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/commandcenter.agent.md))

- **commands**: 저장소를 처음 클론했거나 의존성이 맞지 않을 때는 아래 순서로 초기화합니다. `npm install`로 의존성을 설치한 뒤 `npm run build`로 core → server → client 순서로 전체 빌드를 돌립니다. SQLite 스키마가 아직 없으면 `npm run db:init`으로 초기화하고, 보류 중인 마이그레이션은 `npm run db:migrate`로 적용합니다. ([jee1/memento](https://github.com/jee1/memento/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/commands.md))

- **Comment Sicko**: A deranged comment-hater that savors deletion and condemns workaround code. ([praxstack/markdown-viewer-app](https://github.com/praxstack/markdown-viewer-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/pstack/agents/comment-sicko.md) · ★3 · 11 copies)
- **comment-ablation-auditor**: Adversarial reviewer of one comment-ablation diff. Reads the removed `#` blocks and the surviving ones in an assigned file group, re-derives each deleted fact's recoverability from the code alone, and reports either CLEAN-CUT (lossless) or DEEP-CUT (a fact the code cannot supply, with the keep class it should have survived under) — plus EARNED or UNEARNED for every surviving comment. Read-only: its tool set has no edit capability. Never reads the ablation's own reports, never invokes kata, never fixes anything. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign's review wave. ([taylorsatula/mira](https://github.com/taylorsatula/mira/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/comment-ablation-auditor.md) · ★484)
- **comment-ablator**: Comment-ablation executor for one assignment of files. Reads the comment-ablation skill first, then deletes `#` comments the code already supplies, keeping only the four narrow classes, reporting contradictions and work orders instead of fixing them, and proving every file code-inert with the skill's gate. Reads a baseline before editing so a gate failure is attributable to its own change. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign. Never touches string literals, docstrings, non-comment lines, or AGENTS.md maps; never commits. ([taylorsatula/mira](https://github.com/taylorsatula/mira/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/comment-ablator.md) · ★484)

- **comment-analyzer**: Use this agent during PR review to check that a PR's comments and doc-comments are TRUE. It verifies every load-bearing claim in a changed comment against the code it describes, and flags comments the diff left stale. It does not ask for more comments - this repo's committed CLAUDE.md rules default to no comment, so requests to add or expand one are Suggestions at most. It also enforces the repo's load-bearing markers (@deprecated removal versions, TODO(vNN) forms). Comments that verify are endorsed so the reviewer knows accuracy was checked. Read-only on the sandbox checkout. ([nrwl/nx](https://github.com/nrwl/nx/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/comment-analyzer.md) · ★29k)
- **comment-analyzer**: Analyzes code comments for accuracy, completeness, and long-term value. Verifies comments match actual code behavior. Use after generating documentation, before PRs with comment changes, or when auditing for comment rot. Advisory only. - **comment-reviewer**: Reviews comments and docstrings for accuracy against the code they describe, covering both comments changed by a diff and — the primary case — comments left unchanged while the code beneath them changed. Expands each changed hunk to its enclosing declaration, cross-references the docstring against the current signature and behavior, and uses VCS history to separate stale comments from deliberate ones. Advisory only — never edits code or comments, and never reviews the code's correctness. ([jdh313/jdh-agents](https://github.com/jdh313/jdh-agents/blob/HEAD/marketplaces/claude/plugins/craft/agents/comment-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
